Let current a length n, normalize sum maybe S=1. M=max(c) where c=np.convolve(a,a). We solve LP:
maximize sum g

LP function details:
```
def lp_update(a, max_time? maybe):
    n=len(a); a = np.asarray(a); F=a / a.sum() * n? Use sum=1 gives F=n*a, M_F=n^2*maxConv(a)
    c = np.convolve(a,a)
    M_F = n*n*c.max()
    if not finite return None
    A = convolve_matrix(F)  # L x n, rows k F[k-j] valid
    # Solve linprog max sum x: c_vec=-ones(n)
    res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(L,M_F), bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
    if res.success: Gs = -res.fun; G=res.x
    # Due Feasibility, G may have tiny neg
    G=np.clip(G,0,None); if G.sum<=0: return None
    return G/G.sum()
```

Implement fast objective:
```
def fast_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0 or not finite: return inf
   a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01: return inf
   # Values may >1000, evaluator clips; but we normalize sum=1 so not.
   a=a/ s  # scale invariant; but evaluator sum^2 so OK; sum=1
   if np.max(a)>1000: a=np.minimum(a,1000); s=... but not
   c=np.convolve(a,a, mode='full')
   return 2*a.size*c.max()
```
Scale to sum1 ensures values small. Since evaluator clips >1000 but sum1 values≤1. Good.

Need ensure returned sequence non-negative, list floats. We can return best with values maybe sum1; evaluator ok. If best_score inf fallback parent or ones.

Smooth noise function:
```
def smooth_noise(rng, n, smooth=0.2):
   freq = np.fft.fftfreq(n)
   spec = rng.normal(size=n) + 1j*rng.normal(size=n)
   spec *= np.exp(-smooth * (freq/(2*(n-1)/n))**2) # 
   v=np.fft.irfft(spec, n)
   return v - v.mean()
```
But ensure real. We want anti after reflection: q=v - v[::-1]. If v has DC removed? not necessary. Normalize by L2.

```
Now, let's think about the active gradient direction and gradient scaling. Implement:
```
def gradient_direction(a):
    n=a.size
    c=np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
    M=c.max()
    # choose tau = M * 0.05? c values up to M, some ~1/(2n). For tau too small weights only max; too large broad. Try tau = 0.1*(c[M>0].std? )
    # Use c clipped, tau = 0.1 * max(1e-30, c.max() - c.min()) or 0.2*M?
    tau = 0.2 * max(M, 1e-15)
    w = np.exp((c - M) / tau)
    w /= w.sum()
    wrev = w[::-1]
    conv = np.convolve(a, wrev, mode='full')
    v = conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    grad = 2.0 * v
    mu = float(np.dot(a, grad))
    d = a * (grad - mu)
    norm = np.linalg.norm(d)
    if norm>0: d/=norm
    return d
```
This direction reduces f_smooth? Descent y = y - step*d (since d=∂f/∂y). If d norm1, step 0.1 in y. Could be good. We can also use ascent? No descent. For nonuniform, should improve. We can test mentally? Not.
```

def local_search(a, score, deadline, rng, max_updates=1000, print every 20):
    if not finite: return
    cur=a.copy(); cur_score=score
    best_local=cur.copy(); best_local_score=score
    y=np.log(cur+1e-300)
    T=0.02
    no_imp=0
    while time<deadline and no_imp<50? :
       choose mode alternating
       q=...
       for step in steps:
           yn=y+step*q
           cand=normalize_log(yn)
           sc=safe_score(cand)
           if finite:
              if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                   accept
              elif T>1e-6 and rng.random() < exp((cur_score-sc)/T) and sc > lower? accept
              if accepted:
                   y=np.log(cand+eps); cur=cand; cur_score=sc
                   update best
                   no_imp=0; break
              else if first accepted? continue with larger step? We'll just try steps.
       if not accepted: no_imp+=1; T*=0.95; if no_imp>30 reset y=log(cur+eps); if no_imp>100 break or big reset to best.
    return best_local,best_local_score
```

```
A = conv_matrix(F) # L x n
B = A.T  # n x L

LP step:
```
def lp_step(a, deadline):
    if not have scipy: return None
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: return None
    n=a.size
    # maybe downsample if n>1024? But a already work_n. If n>1024, use work_n?
    F=a/a.sum()*n
    F=np.maximum(F, 1e-12*n) ?
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    M_F=n*n*c.max()
    if not finite M_F or M_F<=0: return None
    L=2*n-1
    if toeplitz:
       ccol=np.empty(L); ccol[:n]=F; ccol[n:]=0
       rrow=np.zeros(n); rrow[0]=F[0]
       A=toeplitz(ccol, rrow)
    else: ...
    cvec=-np.ones(n)
    bvec=np.full(L, M_F)
    try:
       res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
    except Exception:
       return None
    if not res.success or res.x is None: return None
    G=np.clip(res.x,0,None)
    s=G.sum()
    if not finite or s <=0.5*n: maybe if s<=0 return None
    if s <= n*(1+1e-6): return None? Could still useful? If no sum increase, no improvement likely. Return None.
    return G/s
```

```
def lp_polish(best, best_score, deadline):
   cur=best.copy(); cur_score=best_score
   rng=default_rng(1234)
   no=0
   while time<deadline-30:
      G=lp_step(cur,deadline)
      if G is None: break
      # line search
      lam_best=0; sc_best=cur_score
      # evaluate G itself maybe
      candidates=[]
      for lam in [0.03,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]: ...
      # refine around lam_best
      if sc_best < cur_score - 1e-12:
          cur=...; cur_score=sc_best; if cur_score<best_score update; no=0
      else:
          no+=1
          if no>=5: break
   return cur, best...
```

`make_curve` implementation stable:
```
def make_curve(t, alpha, beta, gamma):
   a=np.ones_like(t)
   if alpha !=0:
      a *= np.exp(np.clip(alpha*t, -20, 20))
   if beta !=0:
      # (1-t)**beta; at t=1 zero if beta>0
      factor=np.ones_like(t)
      mask=t<1
      factor[mask]=np.power(1.0-t[mask], beta)
      factor[~mask]=0.0 if beta>0 else 1
      a *= factor
   if gamma !=0:
      mask=t>0
      factor=np.zeros_like(t)
      factor[mask]=np.power(t[mask], gamma)
      if gamma==0: factor[0]=1
      a *= factor
   a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: a=np.exp(np.clip(alpha*t,-20,20)); s=...
   return a/s
```
At t=0 gamma=0, power 0? We set. For beta=0 no factor. For gamma=0 and t=0, no factor. Use small eps for power? `t[mask]` >0.

For beta>0, at t=1 value zero; but if alpha finite. Good.

`normalize_arr`:
```
def normalize_arr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0: return np.ones(256)/256
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size if a.size>0 else 256)/max(a.size,1)
    a = a/s
    # evaluator caps 1000, but sum1 ok
    return a
```
If a has size 0, `a.size if...` careful.

`log_normalize(z)`:
```
def log_normalize(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if z.ndim!=1 or z.size==0: return np.ones(256)/256
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -1000.0)
    zm=z.max()
    e=np.exp(z-zm)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return uniform
    return e/s
```

`safe_score(a)`: If a is None: inf. Normalize? For speed, if a already sum1, but ensure. Using sum may alter if min? For local, a sum1. Could skip sum? Need if bad. We'll normalize to be safe. `n=a.size`. If n<1 return inf. If max(a)>1000? after sum1 no. Conv.
```
c = np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
maxb = float(c.max())
if not finite: return inf
return float(2*n*maxb) # sum=1
```
But if score maybe less than 0 due? no.

```
def parent_sequence():
    try:
       seq = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
       if seq.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(seq)):
           seq=np.clip(seq,0,None)
           s=seq.sum()
           if s>1e-12: return seq/s
    except Exception: pass
    return np.ones(512)/512.0
```

```
def consider_candidate(best,best_score,cand):
   if cand is None: return best,best_score
   cand=normalize_arr(cand)
   sc=safe_score(cand)
   if np.isfinite(sc) and (not np.isfinite(best_score) or sc < best_score - 1e-15):
       return cand, sc
   return best,best_score
```
This normalizes and convolves each candidate. Many times. For internal local, we already have score; but for starts it's fine. Use in prop.

`make_curve` and `poly_profile`.

`make_powers(t, degree)`:
```
def make_powers(t, degree):
    n=t.size
    P=np.empty((n,degree), dtype=float)
    col=np.ones(n)
    for k in range(degree):
       col = col * t
       P[:,k]=col
    return P
```
This gives t^1..t^degree.

`poly_profile(powers, coeff)`:
```
z = powers.dot(coeff)
z = np.clip(z, -18.0, 18.0)
# normalize, optional subtract mean? not necessary but can reduce range
```

```
def optimize_coefficients(x0, powers, deadline):
   coeff=x0.astype(float).copy()
   sc0=score_poly(coeff,powers)
   best=coeff; bestsc=sc0
   if _minimize is not None and time<deadline-60:
      try:
         res=_minimize(score_poly, coeff, args=(powers,), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':800, 'xatol':1e-4, 'fatol':1e-7, 'adaptive':True})
         if res.success or hasattr(res,'fun'):
             if np.isfinite(res.fun) and res.fun < bestsc:
                bestsc=res.fun; best=res.x
      except Exception: pass
   # random hill-climb to polish using remaining time until deadline? We'll pass.
   if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
      rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
      cur=best.copy(); cursc=bestsc; T=0.005
      for it in range(5000):
          if time>deadline: break
          scale=0.3
          prop=cur + rng.normal(size=cur.size)*scale
          prop=np.clip(prop,-15,15)
          psc=score_poly(prop,powers)
          if np.isfinite(psc) and (psc < cursc or rng.random() < np.exp(-max(0,psc-cursc)/max(T,1e-8))):
              cur=prop; cursc=psc
              if psc < bestsc: bestsc=psc; best=prop.copy(); T=max(1e-5,T*0.95)
          else:
              T*=0.98
   best=np.clip(best,-15,15)
   return poly_profile(powers,best), bestsc
```

```
def local_search(a, score, deadline, rng=None, t=None, max_steps=20000):

   cur=a.copy(); cur_score=score

   T=0.01

          elif mode==1: sine combo anti

          elif mode==3: smooth sine symmetric? maybe
          else: gradient direction
```

Make direction top-level helpers:
```
def random_anti_noise(rng,n):
   v=rng.normal(size=n)
   q=v - v[::-1]
   q -= q.mean()
   return q

def sine_anti_direction(rng,n,t,nsines=4):
   x=2*t-1
   q=np.zeros(n)
   for _ in range(nsines):
      k=int(rng.integers(1, 12))
      amp=float(rng.normal())
      phase=float(rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi))
      q += amp*np.sin(np.pi*k*x + phase)
   q-=q.mean(); return q
```
But phase breaks anti? sin(πk(1-x)+phase) = sin(πk -πkx + phase). sin(A-B)=sinA cosB - cosA sinB = sin(phase? not simply -). To preserve anti, need no phase or phase 0/π. With phase, not anti. Use amplitudes with random sign only: `amp = ±abs`. `q += amp * sin(π k x)`. This ensures anti. Good. If want more varied, random k and sign.
- For symmetric modes? Could use cos(π k x) with x=2t-1: cos(πk(1-x))=cos(πk -πkx)=(-1)^k cos(πkx). Not anti. Could include symmetric to shape even features but center increases; maybe with negative amplitudes. We can include a separate symmetric basis direction occasionally. But random high covers.
`q = amp * np.sin(np.pi*k*x)`; k int 1..16.
```
def reflection_anti_direction(z):
   q = z - z[::-1]
   q-=q.mean(); return q
```
This is based on current log asymmetry, normalized.
- Gradient direction top-level:
```
def gradient_direction(a):
   c=np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
   M=float(c.max())
   if M<=0 or not finite: return None
   tau=0.2*M
   w=np.exp((c-M)/tau)
   s=w.sum()
   if s<=0: return None
   w/=s
   wrev=w[::-1]
   conv=np.convolve(a,wrev,mode='full')
   v=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
   grad=2*v
   mu=float(np.dot(a,grad))
   d=a*(grad-mu)
   return d
```
This does an extra conv, used occasionally.

Normalize direction:
```
def normalize_direction(q):
   q=np.asarray(q,float)
   if q.size==0: return None
   q=np.where(np.isfinite(q),q,0)
   q -= q.mean()
   m=max(abs)
   if m<=1e-15: return None
   return q/m
```

Local accept criteria:
```
def maybe_accept(cur_score, cand_score, T, rng):
    if cand_score < cur_score: return True
    if T <=1e-9: return False
    diff=cand_score-cur_score
    if diff > 5*T: return False
    return rng.random() < np.exp(-diff/T)
```
Temperatures: objective around 1.5, useful improvements 0.01. T=0.005. Accepting worse up to 0.03 maybe. Good. Start T=0.01, decrease 0.995 per fail, min 1e-5. If no_improve 50, reset T=0.02.

`lp_step` with time:
```
def lp_step(a, deadline):
   if _linprog is None or _toeplitz is None: return None
   if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180: return None
   n=a.size
   # if n > 1280? maybe downsample? Use min(n,1024) to keep LP fast. But LP on smaller shape then return smaller? We need same length to mix? If downsample, return length smaller. Could mix by interpolation to n. But simpler LP on if n<=1280 else interpolate to 1024, LP, then interpolate result back to n, line search. More complex. Use WORK_N=1536 maybe matrix 38MB okay. So no.
   F = a * n
   F = np.maximum(F, 1e-14*n)
   c=np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
   M_F=n*n*float(c.max())
   if not finite or M_F<=0: return None
   L=2*n-1
   ccol=np.zeros(L); ccol[:n]=F
   rrow=np.zeros(n); rrow[0]=F[0]
   A=_toeplitz(ccol, rrow)
   cvec=-np.ones(n)
   bvec=np.full(L, M_F*(1+1e-12))
   bounds=(0.0, None) # For linprog, bounds can be list or tuple of tuple? Use None? In scipy, bounds=(0,None) works for all? yes if iterable? Actually bounds can be (lb,ub) tuple applies to all? I think yes. Use `bounds=[(0,None)]*n` to be safe.
   res=_linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
   if res is None or not res.success: return None
   G=np.asarray(res.x,float)
   G=np.clip(G,0,None)
   s=float(G.sum())
   if not finite or s <= 0.5*n: return None
   if s <= n*(1+1e-7): return None
   return G/s
```
Bounds list creation O(n) fine. If _toeplitz not available, build manually. If MemoryError, return None.

```
def lp_line_search(cur, G, cur_score):
   candidates=[]

      # refine near best_lam: sample between previous/next? simple local
      centers=[best_lam-0.05, best_lam-0.02, best_lam, best_lam+0.02, best_lam+0.05]
      for lam in centers:
         if 0<lam<1: ...
   return best_mix,best_sc,best_lam
```

```
def lp_polish(a,score,deadline):
   cur=a.copy(); cur_score=score; best=a.copy(); best_score=score; no=0; it=0; rng=default_rng(555)
   while time<deadline-60:
      G=lp_step(cur,deadline)
      if G is None: break
      mix,sc,lam=lp_line_search(cur,G,cur_score)
      if lam>0 and sc < cur_score - 1e-10:
          cur=mix; cur_score=sc; no=0
          if sc < best_score:
              best=cur.copy(); best_score=sc; print
      else:
          no +=1
          if no>=4:
              # perturb current with G maybe

                 cur=normalize_arr(0.5*cur+0.5*G); cur_score=safe_score(cur)
                 no=0
              else: break
      it+=1
      if it%3==0 and no>0:
         # small local? skip
   return best,best_score
```

Need `interpolate_length` top-level:
```
def interpolate_length(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==n: return a.copy()
   if a.size<2: return np.full(n,a.mean())? normalize
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   return normalize_arr(y)
```

Let's write `update_best_state`:
```
def update_best_state(best,best_score,candidates,cand,score):
    if cand is None: return best,best_score,candidates
    cand_arr = normalize_arr(cand)
    sc = score
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = safe_score(cand_arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return best,best_score,candidates
    # maybe if cand_arr.sum isn't 1? normalize did
    if sc < best_score - 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best = cand_arr
        best_score = sc
        print('new best', sc)
    # candidates list
    candidates = list(candidates)
    for i in range(len(candidates)):
       if sc < candidates[i][1] - 1e-12:
          candidates.insert(i,(cand_arr,sc)); break
    else:
       candidates.append((cand_arr,sc))
    if len(candidates) > 6:
       candidates.pop()
    return best,best_score,candidates
```
Printing many could be verbose but okay. Maybe use only when new best.

```
def pop_powers(t, degree):
    n = int(t.size)
    P = np.empty((n, degree), dtype=np.float64)
    col = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    tt = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(degree):
        col = col * tt
        P[:, k] = col
    return P
```

`make_curve`:
```
def make_curve(t, alpha, beta, gamma):
    t = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.ones_like(t)
    if alpha != 0.0:
        a *= np.exp(np.clip(alpha * t, -20.0, 20.0))
    if beta != 0.0:
        fac = np.zeros_like(t)
        m = t < 1.0
        fac[m] = np.power(1.0 - t[m], beta)
        if beta == 0.0: fac[~m]=1? no
        # At t=1, fac=0 if beta>0
        a *= fac
    if gamma != 0.0:
        fac = np.zeros_like(t)
        m = t > 0.0
        fac[m] = np.power(t[m], gamma)
        # at t=0 fac=0
        a *= fac
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.exp(np.clip(alpha * t, -20.0, 20.0))
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            a = np.ones_like(t)
            s = float(a.size)
    return a / s
```
If beta>0, at t=1 fac zero. If both beta,gamma positive all a maybe zero? e.g., alpha 0, beta=1,gamma=1 t=0 and1 zeros, interior positive. fine. If t has only endpoints n=1, zero; n>1 okay. If n=2 and beta,gamma positive, all zero? t=[0,1], both zero -> fallback alpha. Good.

```
def parametric_starts(n, deadline, best, best_score, candidates):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    alphas=[-1.0,-0.5,0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5]
    betas=[0.0,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0]
    gammas=[0.0,0.25,0.5,1.0]

       for be in betas:
```

```python
"""
Hybrid search for nonnegative coefficient sequences minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The implementation combines:
  1. Smooth parametric starts (exponential/Beta-like curves and log-polynomials).
  2. Low-dimensional coefficient optimization of log-polynomial profiles.
  3. Simulated-annealing style local search in log-space.
  4. Linear-programming feasibility updates inspired by the previous optimum:
     for fixed f, maximize sum(g) subject to f*g <= max(f*f), then mix.
A valid sequence is always returned before the deadline.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.linalg import toeplitz as _toeplitz
except Exception:
    _toeplitz = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _minimize
except Exception:
    _linprog = None
    _minimize = None


TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027
WORK_N = 1536
COEF_N = 768
LONG_N = 3072
EPS = 1e-300


def parent_sequence():
    """Return the supplied height_sequence_1, normalized and guarded."""
    try:
        seq = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if seq.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(seq)):
            seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, None)
            s = float(seq.sum())
            if s > 1e-12:
                seq = seq / s
                if seq.size > 20000:
                    seq = interpolate_length(seq, 4096)
                return seq
    except Exception:
        pass
    return np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64) / 512.0


def normalize_arr(a):
    """Convert to a valid normalized nonnegative vector."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def interpolate_length(a, n):
    """Linearly resample a profile to length n."""
    a = normalize_arr(a)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return normalize_arr(a)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]))
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n) / n
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(xn, xo, a)
    return normalize_arr(y)


def score_unit(a):
    """Fast exact score assuming a is already normalized to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf
    c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(a.size) * m


def safe_score(a):
    """Score with validation and normalization."""
    a = normalize_arr(a)
    return score_unit(a)


def make_powers(t, degree):
    """Power basis t^1, ..., t^degree."""
    t = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(t.size)
    degree = int(degree)
    if degree <= 0:
        return np.empty((n, 0), dtype=np.float64)
    P = np.empty((n, degree), dtype=np.float64)
    col = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(degree):
        col = col * t
        P[:, k] = col
    return P


def poly_profile(powers, coeff):
    """Positive profile exp(sum coeff[k] t^(k+1)), normalized."""
    coeff = np.asarray(coeff, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if powers.size == 0 or coeff.size == 0:
        n = powers.shape[0] if powers.shape[0] else 1
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    z = powers.dot(coeff)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -18.0, 18.0)
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(powers.shape[0], dtype=np.float64) / float(powers.shape[0])
    return a / s


def score_poly(coeff, powers):
    """Objective for a coefficient vector."""
    return score_unit(poly_profile(powers, coeff))


def fit_coeffs(a, powers):
    """Least-squares fit of log(a) to power basis."""
    a = normalize_arr(a)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, EPS))
    loga = loga - float(np.mean(loga))
    try:
        c = np.linalg.lstsq(powers, loga, rcond=None)[0]
        return np.clip(c, -18.0, 18.0)
    except Exception:
        return np.zeros(powers.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)


def make_curve(t, alpha, beta, gamma):
    """
    Profile proportional to exp(alpha*t) * (1-t)^beta * t^gamma.
    Allows endpoint tapering and skewing.
    """
    t = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.ones_like(t, dtype=np.float64)

    if alpha != 0.0:
        a *= np.exp(np.clip(alpha * t, -20.0, 20.0))

    if beta != 0.0:
        fac = np.zeros_like(t, dtype=np.float64)
        m = t < 1.0
        fac[m] = np.power(1.0 - t[m], beta)
        a *= fac

    if gamma != 0.0:
        fac = np.zeros_like(t, dtype=np.float64)
        m = t > 0.0
        fac[m] = np.power(t[m], gamma)
        a *= fac

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        # Fallback: plain exponential / uniform.
        if alpha != 0.0:
            a = np.exp(np.clip(alpha * t, -20.0, 20.0))
            s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            a = np.ones_like(t, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, cand, score):
    """
    Keep the global best and a small list of diverse good candidates.
    The passed score is trusted if finite; otherwise it is recomputed.
    """
    if cand is None:
        return best, best_score, candidates

    arr = normalize_arr(cand)
    try:
        sc = float(score)
    except Exception:
        sc = np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = safe_score(arr)

    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return best, best_score, candidates

    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or sc < best_score - 1e-12:
        best = arr.copy()
        best_score = sc
        print(f"new best: n={arr.size}, score={sc:.10f}, time={time.monotonic():.1f}")

    candidates = list(candidates)
    inserted = False
    for i, (_, s) in enumerate(candidates):
        if sc < s - 1e-12:
            candidates.insert(i, (arr.copy(), sc))
            inserted = True
            break
    if not inserted:
        candidates.append((arr.copy(), sc))
    while len(candidates) > 6:
        candidates.pop()
    return best, best_score, candidates


def random_anti_noise(rng, n):
    """Random anti-palindromic direction."""
    v = rng.normal(size=n)
    q = v - v[::-1]
    q -= float(np.mean(q))
    return q


def random_sine_anti(rng, n, t):
    """Random anti-symmetric smooth sine direction."""
    x = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    q = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(4):
        k = int(rng.integers(1, 18))
        amp = float(rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0])) * float(abs(rng.normal()) + 0.25)
        q += amp * np.sin(np.pi * k * x)
    q -= float(np.mean(q))
    return q


def reflection_direction(z):
    """Direction that amplifies anti-palindromic asymmetry in log-space."""
    q = z - z[::-1]
    q -= float(np.mean(q))
    return q


def normalize_direction(q):
    if q is None:
        return None
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if q.size == 0:
        return None
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q -= float(np.mean(q))
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(q))) if q.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-14:
        return None
    return q / m


def gradient_direction(a):
    """
    Subgradient-like direction from a smoothed-max objective.
    Uses softmax weights over convolution values.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None
    c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    tau = 0.25 * M
    w = np.exp((c - M) / tau)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    w = w / s
    wrev = w[::-1]
    conv = np.convolve(a, wrev, mode="full")
    v = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    grad = 2.0 * v
    mu = float(np.dot(a, grad))
    d = a * (grad - mu)
    return d


def local_search(a, score, deadline, rng, t=None):
    """
    Log-space simulated-annealing local search.
    Keeps a local best and returns it.
    """
    a = normalize_arr(a)
    try:
        score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        score = np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = score_unit(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return a, score

    n = a.size
    if t is None or t.size != n:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    cur = a.copy()
    cur_score = score
    best_local = cur.copy()
    best_local_score = score

    z = np.log(cur + EPS)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))

    T = 0.01
    no_imp = 0
    it = 0
    steps = (0.05, 0.15, 0.40, 1.00)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_imp < 500:
        it += 1

        q = None
        if (it % 7) == 3:
            q = normalize_direction(gradient_direction(cur))
        else:
            mode = it % 4
            if mode == 0:
                q = normalize_direction(random_anti_noise(rng, n))
            elif mode == 1:
                q = normalize_direction(random_sine_anti(rng, n, t))
            elif mode == 2:
                q = normalize_direction(reflection_direction(z))
            else:
                q = normalize_direction(rng.normal(size=n) - rng.normal(size=n)[::-1])

        if q is None:
            no_imp += 1
            continue

        best_cand = None
        best_sc = np.inf
        best_z = None

        for step in steps:
            zc = z + step * q
            try:
                zmax = float(np.max(zc))
                e = np.exp(zc - zmax)
                s = float(e.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                    continue
                cand = e / s
            except Exception:
                continue
            sc = score_unit(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_cand = cand
                best_z = zc

        if best_cand is None:
            no_imp += 1
            continue

        accept = False
        if best_sc < cur_score - 1e-13:
            accept = True
        elif T > 1e-9:
            diff = best_sc - cur_score
            if diff <= 40.0 * T:
                try:
                    accept = rng.random() < np.exp(-diff / T)
                except Exception:
                    accept = False

        if accept:
            cur = best_cand
            cur_score = best_sc
            z = best_z
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
            no_imp = 0
            T = max(1e-6, T * 0.92)
            if cur_score < best_local_score - 1e-13:
                best_local = cur.copy()
                best_local_score = cur_score
                if it % 500 == 0:
                    print(f"local update: score={best_local_score:.10f}, it={it}")
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            T = max(1e-6, T * 0.997)
            if no_imp == 60:
                cur = best_local.copy()
                cur_score = best_local_score
                z = np.log(cur + EPS)
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                T = max(T, 0.02)

    return best_local, best_local_score


def optimize_coefficients(x0, powers, deadline):
    """
    Optimize a low-dimensional log-polynomial profile.
    Returns (profile_at_powers_length, score, coeff).
    """
    x0 = np.clip(np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), -18.0, 18.0).copy()

    cur = x0
    try:
        cur_score = score_poly(cur, powers)
    except Exception:
        cur_score = np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        cur = np.zeros_like(x0)
        cur_score = score_poly(cur, powers)

    best_coeff = cur.copy()
    best_score = cur_score
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)

    # Short time-safe random hill-climb / anneal.
    T = 0.005
    no_imp = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 30 and no_imp < 700:
        prop = cur + rng.normal(size=cur.size) * 0.30
        prop = np.clip(prop, -18.0, 18.0)
        try:
            psc = score_poly(prop, powers)
        except Exception:
            psc = np.inf

        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(psc):
            if psc < cur_score - 1e-13:
                accept = True
            elif T > 1e-9:
                diff = psc - cur_score
                if diff <= 30.0 * T:
                    accept = rng.random() < np.exp(-diff / T)

        if accept:
            cur = prop
            cur_score = psc
            no_imp = 0
            T = max(1e-6, T * 0.98)
            if psc < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = psc
                best_coeff = prop.copy()
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            T = max(1e-7, T * 0.998)

    # Structured local optimization if SciPy is available.
    if _minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 40:
        try:
            res = _minimize(
                score_poly,
                best_coeff.copy(),
                args=(powers,),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={"maxiter": 120, "xatol": 1e-4, "fatol": 1e-7},
            )
            if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
                rx = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), -18.0, 18.0)
                try:
                    rsc = score_poly(rx, powers)
                except Exception:
                    rsc = np.inf
                if np.isfinite(rsc) and rsc < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_score = rsc
                    best_coeff = rx
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_a = poly_profile(powers, best_coeff)
    return best_a, best_score, best_coeff


def run_coefficient_stage(t_work, best, best_score, candidates, deadline):
    """Low-dimensional profile optimization, evaluated at several modest lengths."""
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return best, best_score, candidates

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240611)

    for degree in (8, 11):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        t_c = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, COEF_N, dtype=np.float64)
        powers_c = make_powers(t_c, degree)
        powers_w = make_powers(t_work, degree)

        # Start from current best, fitted to coefficients.
        a_base = interpolate_length(best, COEF_N)
        x0 = fit_coeffs(a_base, powers_c)
        a_c, sc_c, coeff = optimize_coefficients(x0, powers_c, min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 90))
        if np.isfinite(sc_c):
            best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, a_c, sc_c)
            if coeff is not None and coeff.size:
                a_w = poly_profile(powers_w, coeff)
                sc_w = score_unit(a_w)
                best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, a_w, sc_w)

        # A couple of random coefficient restarts.
        for restart in range(2):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            xr = rng.normal(size=degree) * 0.45
            xr[0] += rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0]) * 0.5
            a_c, sc_c, coeff = optimize_coefficients(xr, powers_c, min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 45))
            if np.isfinite(sc_c):
                best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, a_c, sc_c)
                if coeff is not None and coeff.size:
                    a_w = poly_profile(powers_w, coeff)
                    sc_w = score_unit(a_w)
                    best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, a_w, sc_w)

    return best, best_score, candidates


def build_convolution_matrix(F):
    """
    Build dense matrix A with A[k,j] = F[k-j] when 0 <= k-j < len(F), else 0.
    This is the full convolution matrix for F * G.
    """
    F = np.asarray(F, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = F.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if _toeplitz is not None:
        c = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        c[:n] = F
        r = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        r[0] = F[0]
        A = _toeplitz(c, r)
        A = np.asarray(A, dtype=np.float64)
        if A.shape == (L, n):
            return A

    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    cols = np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
    for d, val in enumerate(F):
        cnt = n - d
        if cnt <= 0:
            continue
        rows = cols[:cnt] + d
        A[rows, cols[:cnt]] = val
    return A


def lp_step(a, deadline):
    """
    LP update: solve max sum(G) s.t. F*G <= max(F*F), G>=0.
    Returns normalized G if sum(G) exceeds current scale, else None.
    """
    if _linprog is None:
        return None
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 180:
        return None

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return None

    F = a * float(n)
    F = np.maximum(F, 1e-14 * float(n))
    c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    M_F = float(n * n * np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M_F) or M_F <= 0.0:
        return None

    try:
        A = build_convolution_matrix(F)
        L = 2 * n - 1
        b = np.full(L, M_F * (1.0 + 1e-12), dtype=np.float64)
        cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

        try:
            res = _linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            res = _linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs")

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            return None

        G = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        G = np.clip(G, 0.0, None)
        s = float(G.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.5 * n:
            return None
        if s <= n * (1.0 + 1e-7):
            return None
        return G / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def lp_line_search(cur, G, cur_score):
    """Choose a convex mixture of cur and the LP-generated G."""
    best_lam = 0.0
    best_mix = cur.copy()
    best_sc = cur_score

    for lam in (0.03, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00):
        mix = (1.0 - lam) * cur + lam * G
        mix = normalize_arr(mix)
        sc = score_unit(mix)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best_sc = sc
            best_lam = lam
            best_mix = mix

    if best_lam > 0.0:
        for lam in (best_lam - 0.05, best_lam - 0.02, best_lam + 0.02, best_lam + 0.05):
            if lam <= 0.0 or lam >= 1.0:
                continue
            mix = (1.0 - lam) * cur + lam * G
            mix = normalize_arr(mix)
            sc = score_unit(mix)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best_sc = sc
                best_lam = lam
                best_mix = mix
    return best_mix, best_sc, best_lam


def lp_polish(a, score, deadline):
    """Iterate LP update and line search until time or stagnation."""
    cur = normalize_arr(a)
    try:
        cur_score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        cur_score = np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        cur_score = safe_score(cur)
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        return cur, cur_score

    best = cur.copy()
    best_score = cur_score
    no_imp = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
        G = lp_step(cur, deadline)
        if G is None:
            break

        mix, sc, lam = lp_line_search(cur, G, cur_score)

        if lam > 0.0 and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
            cur = mix
            cur_score = sc
            no_imp = 0
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = cur.copy()
                best_score = cur_score
                print(f"LP improvement: score={best_score:.10f}, lam={lam:.3f}")
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 5:
                break
    return best, best_score


def build_parametric_starts(n, deadline):
    """Generate deterministic and random smooth starts."""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    starts = []

    alphas = (-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00, 1.25, 1.50)
    betas = (0.0, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00, 1.50, 2.00)
    gammas = (0.0, 0.25, 0.50, 1.00)

    for al in alphas:
        for be in betas:
            for ga in gammas:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    return starts
                a = make_curve(t, al, be, ga)
                sc = score_unit(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    starts.append((a, sc))
                    b = a[::-1]
                    scb = score_unit(b)
                    if np.isfinite(scb):
                        starts.append((b, scb))

    # Random log-polynomial starts.
    powers = make_powers(t, 6)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(13579)
    for _ in range(35):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        c = rng.normal(size=6) * 0.35
        c[0] += rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0]) * 0.6
        a = poly_profile(powers, c)
        sc = score_unit(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            starts.append((a, sc))
        ar = a[::-1]
        scr = score_unit(ar)
        if np.isfinite(scr):
            starts.append((ar, scr))

    starts.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
    if len(starts) > 12:
        starts = starts[:12]
    return starts


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a good nonnegative sequence and return it as a list of floats.
    """
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Base valid fallback.
    best = parent_sequence()
    best = normalize_arr(best)
    best_score = safe_score(best)
    candidates = []
    best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, best, best_score)

    # Add a resampled version of the parent if it may encode useful structure.
    try:
        if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score <= 2.20:
            p_work = interpolate_length(best, WORK_N)
            sc = score_unit(p_work)
            best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, p_work, sc)
    except Exception:
        pass

    t_work = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, WORK_N, dtype=np.float64)

    # Phase 1: smooth parametric/random starts.
    param_deadline = min(deadline, start_time + 120.0)
    for cand, sc in build_parametric_starts(WORK_N, param_deadline):
        best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, cand, sc)

    # Phase 2: low-dimensional coefficient optimization.
    coef_deadline = min(deadline, start_time + 360.0)
    if time.monotonic() < coef_deadline - 30:
        best, best_score, candidates = run_coefficient_stage(
            t_work, best, best_score, candidates, coef_deadline
        )

    # Phase 3: local search on a few promising starts, then on global best.
    local_end = min(deadline, start_time + 900.0)
    if time.monotonic() < local_end - 30 and candidates:
        chosen = list(candidates)[:3]
        rem = max(0.0, local_end - time.monotonic())
        for i, (cand, sc) in enumerate(chosen):
            if time.monotonic() >= local_end - 15:
                break
            slice_end = time.monotonic() + rem / float(len(chosen) - i)
            slice_end = min(slice_end, local_end)
            a_loc, sc_loc = local_search(cand, sc, slice_end, rng, t_work)
            best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, a_loc, sc_loc)

    if time.monotonic() < local_end - 15:
        a_loc, sc_loc = local_search(best, best_score, local_end, rng, t_work)
        best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, a_loc, sc_loc)

    # Phase 4: LP polishing.
    lp_end = min(deadline, start_time + 1450.0)
    if time.monotonic() < lp_end - 60 and _linprog is not None:
        a_lp, sc_lp = lp_polish(best, best_score, lp_end)
        best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, a_lp, sc_lp)

    # Phase 5: final local refinement.
    local2_end = min(deadline, start_time + 1750.0)
    if time.monotonic() < local2_end - 20:
        a_loc, sc_loc = local_search(best, best_score, local2_end, rng, t_work)
        best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, a_loc, sc_loc)

    # Phase 6: optionally upsample to a longer sequence for final fine search.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
        long_n = LONG_N
        if long_n > WORK_N:
            a_long = interpolate_length(best, long_n)
            sc_long = safe_score(a_long)
            best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, a_long, sc_long)
            if np.isfinite(sc_long) and sc_long < 2.05 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25:
                t_long = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, long_n, dtype=np.float64)
                a_long, sc_long = local_search(best, best_score, deadline - 5.0, rng, t_long)
                best, best_score, candidates = update_best_state(best, best_score, candidates, a_long, sc_long)

    # Final guard: return a finite, normalized, nonnegative list.
    best = normalize_arr(best)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best)) or best.size == 0:
        best = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0

    try:
        exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best])
        if np.isfinite(exact):
            pass
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in best]
```
